- **moonwalk**: Walk an artifact BACKWARD through time to recover, with evidence, WHY it exists — provenance archaeology + a Chesterton's-Fence verdict (KNOWN-DEAD / KNOWN-LIVE / UNKNOWN) that gates a delete or a guarded edit. The evidence-gatherer /raze's fence-gate needs and lacks: it makes "tests pass, cut it" survivable by surfacing the untested failure mode a guard encodes. Reconstructs the cross-store link nobody wrote; cites or declares UNKNOWN, never infers. Read-only except an approved pin. Modes: &lt;target&gt; (recover + verdict) | audit &lt;file|module&gt;. ([yiyaw-lab/agent-armor](https://github.com/yiyaw-lab/agent-armor/tree/HEAD/moonwalk) · ★1)
